 1063|       |/// Execute one length-delimited JSON request. The returned buffer must be freed
 1064|       |/// with `saib_anoncred_free` using the exact returned length.
 1065|       |///
 1066|       |/// # Safety
 1067|       |///
 1068|       |/// `input` must reference `input_len` readable bytes. `output` and `output_len`
 1069|       |/// must be valid writable pointers. On success, ownership of the returned
 1070|       |/// buffer passes to the caller and it must be released exactly once.

 1119|       |/// # Safety

  313|      8|fn fresh_opening(
  314|      8|    label: &str,
  315|      8|    value: u64,
  316|      8|    message_domain: &[u8],
  317|      8|    blinder_domain: &[u8],
  318|      8|) -> CreditCommitmentOpening {

  320|      8|    let blinder_generator = create_domain_proof_generator(blinder_domain);

  322|      8|    let blinder = Scalar::random(OsRng);

  324|      8|    debug_assert!(!label.is_empty());

  336|     12|fn public_range_commitment(
  337|     12|    label: &str,
  338|     12|    opening: &CreditCommitmentOpening,
  339|     12|    lower: u64,
  340|     12|    upper: u64,
  341|     12|    parameter_digest: &str,
  342|     12|) -> Result<PublicRangeCommitment, ApiError> {
  343|     12|    let value = u64::try_from(opening.logical_value)
  344|     12|        .map_err(|_| ApiError::crypto("committed logical value is negative"))?;
                                   ^0                                                      ^0
  345|     12|    if value < lower || value > upper {
  346|      0|        return Err(ApiError::request("value is outside its committed range"));
  347|     12|    }
  348|     12|    let lower_delta = value - lower;
  349|     12|    let upper_delta = upper - value;
  350|     12|    let bp_gens = BulletproofGens::new(64, 2);
  351|     12|    let pc_gens = PedersenGens {
  352|     12|        B: opening.message_generator,
  353|     12|        B_blinding: opening.blinder_generator,
  354|     12|    };
  355|     12|    let mut transcript = range_transcript(label, lower, upper, parameter_digest);
  356|     12|    let (proof, commitments) = RangeProof::prove_multiple(
  357|     12|        &bp_gens,
  358|     12|        &pc_gens,
  359|     12|        &mut transcript,
  360|     12|        &[lower_delta, upper_delta],
  361|     12|        &[opening.blinder, -opening.blinder],
  362|     12|        64,
  363|     12|    )
  364|     12|    .map_err(|error| ApiError::crypto(format!("create {label} range proof: {error:?}")))?;
                                   ^0                                                                 ^0
  365|     12|    let expected = [
  366|     12|        opening.commitment
  367|     12|            - opening.message_generator * encoded_bound(lower, opening.value_encoding)?,
                                                                                                    ^0
  368|     12|        opening.message_generator * encoded_bound(upper, opening.value_encoding)?
                                                                                              ^0
  369|     12|            - opening.commitment,
  370|     12|    ];
  371|     12|    if commitments.as_slice() != expected {
  372|      0|        return Err(ApiError::crypto("range proof commitment relation mismatch"));
  373|     12|    }
  374|     12|    let proof_bytes = serde_bare::to_vec(&proof)
  375|     12|        .map_err(|error| ApiError::crypto(format!("serialize range proof: {error}")))?;
                                       ^0                                                          ^0
  376|     12|    Ok(PublicRangeCommitment {
  377|     12|        label: label.into(),
  378|     12|        message_generator_b64: point_to_b64(opening.message_generator),
  379|     12|        blinder_generator_b64: point_to_b64(opening.blinder_generator),
  380|     12|        commitment_b64: point_to_b64(opening.commitment),
  381|     12|        value_encoding: opening.value_encoding.into(),
  382|     12|        lower,
  383|     12|        upper,
  384|     12|        range_proof_b64: B64.encode(proof_bytes),
  385|     12|    })

  439|     12|fn split_opening(
  440|     12|    label: &str,
  441|     12|    opening: &CreditCommitmentOpening,
  442|     12|) -> Result<(ComponentCommitmentSet, [Vec<SecretComponentOpening>; 3]), ApiError> {
  443|     12|    let value0 = Scalar::random(OsRng);
  444|     12|    let value1 = Scalar::random(OsRng);
  445|     12|    let values = [value0, value1, opening.message - value0 - value1];
  446|     12|    let blind0 = Scalar::random(OsRng);
  447|     12|    let blind1 = Scalar::random(OsRng);
  448|     12|    let blinders = [blind0, blind1, opening.blinder - blind0 - blind1];
  449|     36|    let commitments: [G1Projective; 3] = std::array::from_fn(|index| {
  450|     36|        opening.message_generator * values[index] + opening.blinder_generator * blinders[index]
  451|     36|    });
  452|     12|    if commitments.iter().copied().sum::<G1Projective>() != opening.commitment {
  453|      0|        return Err(ApiError::crypto(
  454|      0|            "replicated VSS commitments do not aggregate",
  455|      0|        ));
  456|     12|    }
  457|     12|    let public = ComponentCommitmentSet {
  458|     12|        label: label.into(),
  459|     12|        commitments_b64: commitments.map(point_to_b64),
  460|     12|    };
  461|     12|    let layouts = [[0usize, 1usize], [1, 2], [2, 0]];
  462|     36|    let nodes = std::array::from_fn(|node| {
  463|     36|        layouts[node]
  464|     36|            .iter()
  465|     72|            .map(|index| SecretComponentOpening {
  466|     72|                label: label.into(),
  467|     72|                component_index: *index as u8,
  468|     72|                value_scalar_b64: scalar_to_b64(values[*index]),
  469|     72|                blinder_scalar_b64: scalar_to_b64(blinders[*index]),
  470|     72|            })
  471|     36|            .collect()
  472|     36|    });
  473|     12|    Ok((public, nodes))

  551|      4|fn parameter_digest(

  553|      4|    policy_digest: &str,
  554|      4|    openings: &[&CreditCommitmentOpening],
  555|      4|) -> Result<String, ApiError> {

  572|    117|fn parameter_digest_from_public(
  573|    117|    bundle: &ConsistencyProofBundle,
  574|    117|    policy: &RangePolicy,
  575|    117|) -> Result<String, ApiError> {
  576|    117|    if bundle.price.lower != policy.price_min
  577|    117|        || bundle.price.upper != policy.price_max

   74|       |    /// as a number claim with the value of `YYYYMMDD`.
   75|      0|    pub fn parse_rfc3339_date<S: AsRef<str>>(date: S) -> Result<Self, Error> {
   76|       |        // Use chrono to check whether the date is valid.
   77|       |        // Invalid dates include days greater than 31, months greater than 12, etc.
   78|       |        // and checks whether the month even supports the number of days specified
   79|       |        // like February 30th is never valid.
   80|      0|        let dt = chrono::NaiveDate::parse_from_str(date.as_ref(), "%Y-%m-%d")
   81|      0|            .map_err(|_| Error::InvalidClaimData("Invalid RFC3339 date"))?;
   82|       |        // There's no need to zero center unless we're dealing with a dates BC
   83|      0|        let mut value = dt.year().to_string();
   84|      0|        if dt.month() < 10 {
   85|      0|            value.push('0');
   86|      0|        }
   87|      0|        value.push_str(&dt.month().to_string());
   88|      0|        if dt.day() < 10 {
   89|      0|            value.push('0');
   90|      0|        }
   91|      0|        value.push_str(&dt.day().to_string());
   92|      0|        Ok(Self::from(value.parse::<isize>().map_err(|_| {
   93|      0|            Error::InvalidClaimData("Invalid RFC3339 date")
   94|      0|        })?))
   95|      0|    }
   96|       |
   97|       |    /// RFC3339 DateTimes are in the format of `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ` and are treated
   98|       |    /// as a number claim representing the number of seconds since the Unix epoch.
   99|      0|    pub fn parse_rfc3339_datetime<S: AsRef<str>>(datetime: S) -> Result<Self, Error> {

   38|       |pub enum ClaimValidator {
   39|       |    /// The Hashed claim data length must be between `min` and `max`
   40|       |    /// `min` default is 0
   41|       |    /// `max` default is [`u64::MAX`]

   49|       |    },
   50|       |    /// The Number claim data length must be between `min` and `max`

   52|       |    /// `max` default is [`i64::MAX`]

   54|       |        /// The minimum inclusive value

   56|       |        min: Option<isize>,
   57|       |        /// The maximum inclusive value
   58|       |        #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   59|       |        max: Option<isize>,
   60|       |    },
   61|       |    /// The Hashed claim data must match this regular expression pattern
   62|       |    #[serde(with = "serde_regex")]
   63|       |    Regex(Regex),
   64|       |    /// The claim data must be one of these
   65|       |    AnyOne(Vec<ClaimData>),
   66|       |}
   67|       |
   68|       |impl ClaimValidator {
   69|       |    /// [`Some(true)`] if the claim is the right type and meets the validator requirements
   70|       |    /// [`Some(false)`] if the claim is the right type but doesn't meet the requirements
   71|       |    /// [`None`] if the claim is the incorrect type
   72|      0|    pub fn is_valid(&self, claim: &ClaimData) -> Option<bool> {
   73|      0|        match self {
   74|      0|            Self::Length { min, max } => match claim {
   75|      0|                ClaimData::Hashed(h) => {
   76|      0|                    let min = min.unwrap_or(0);
   77|      0|                    let max = max.unwrap_or(u64::MAX as usize);
   78|      0|                    let len = h.value.len();
   79|      0|                    Some(min <= len && len <= max)
   80|       |                }
   81|      0|                ClaimData::Revocation(h) => {
   82|      0|                    let min = min.unwrap_or(0);
   83|      0|                    let max = max.unwrap_or(u64::MAX as usize);
   84|      0|                    let len = h.value.len();
   85|      0|                    Some(min <= len && len <= max)
   86|       |                }
   87|      0|                _ => None,
   88|       |            },
   89|      0|            Self::Range { min, max } => match claim {
   90|      0|                ClaimData::Number(n) => {
   91|      0|                    let min = min.unwrap_or(i64::MIN as isize);
   92|      0|                    let max = max.unwrap_or(i64::MAX as isize);
   93|      0|                    Some(min <= n.value && n.value <= max)
   94|       |                }
   95|      0|                _ => None,

  103|      0|                _ => None,
  104|       |            },
  105|      0|            Self::AnyOne(claims) => Some(claims.iter().any(|c| c == claim)),
  106|       |        }
  107|      0|    }

   77|       |    /// Create the Batch Polynomial coefficients
   78|      0|    pub fn create_coefficients(
   79|      0|        &self,
   80|      0|        additions: &[Element],
   81|      0|        deletions: &[Element],
   82|      0|    ) -> Vec<Element> {
   83|      0|        // vD(x) = ∑^{m}_{s=1}{ ∏ 1..s {yD_i + alpha}^-1 ∏ 1 ..s-1 {yD_j - x}
   84|      0|        let one = Scalar::ONE;
   85|      0|        let m1 = -one;
   86|      0|        let mut v_d = Polynomial::with_capacity(deletions.len());
   87|      0|        for s in 0..deletions.len() {
   88|       |            // ∏ 1..s (yD_i + alpha)^-1
   89|      0|            let c = self.batch_deletions(&deletions[0..s + 1]).0;
   90|      0|            let mut poly = Polynomial::with_capacity(deletions.len());
   91|      0|            poly.push(one);
   92|       |            // ∏ 1..(s-1) (yD_j - x)
   93|      0|            for j in deletions.iter().take(s) {
   94|      0|                poly *= &[j.0, m1];
   95|      0|            }
   96|      0|            poly *= c;
   97|      0|            v_d += poly;
   98|       |        }
   99|       |
  100|       |        //v_d(x) * ∏ 1..n (yA_i + alpha)
  101|      0|        v_d *= self.batch_additions(additions).0;
  102|      0|
  103|      0|        // vA(x) = ∑^n_{s=1}{ ∏ 1..s-1 {yA_i + alpha} ∏ s+1..n {yA_j - x} }
  104|      0|        let mut v_a = Polynomial::with_capacity(additions.len());
  105|      0|        for s in 0..additions.len() {
  106|       |            // ∏ 1..s-1 {yA_i + alpha}
  107|      0|            let c = if s == 0 {
  108|      0|                one

  110|      0|                self.batch_additions(&additions[0..s]).0
  111|       |            };
  112|      0|            let mut poly = Polynomial::with_capacity(additions.len());
  113|      0|            poly.push(one);
  114|       |            // ∏ s+1..n {yA_j - x}
  115|      0|            for j in additions.iter().skip(s + 1) {
  116|      0|                poly *= &[j.0, m1];
  117|      0|            }
  118|      0|            poly *= c;
  119|      0|            v_a += poly;
  120|       |        }
  121|       |        // vA - vD
  122|      0|        v_a -= v_d;
  123|      0|
  124|      0|        v_a.0.iter().map(|b| Element(*b)).collect()
  125|      0|    }

  103|       |    /// Create a new membership proof committing phase
  104|     68|    pub fn new(
  105|     68|        y: ProofMessage<Scalar>,
  106|     68|        witness: MembershipWitness,
  107|     68|        proof_params: ProofParams,
  108|     68|        pubkey: PublicKey,
  109|     68|    ) -> Self {
  110|     68|        let message = y.get_message();
  111|     68|        let rng = rand_core::OsRng;
  112|     68|        // Randomly select σ, ρ
  113|     68|        let sigma = generate_fr(SALT, None, rng);
  114|     68|        let rho = generate_fr(SALT, None, rng);
  115|     68|
  116|     68|        // E_C = C + (σ + ρ)Z
  117|     68|        let e_c = proof_params.z * (sigma + rho) + witness.0;
  118|     68|
  119|     68|        // T_σ = σX
  120|     68|        let t_sigma = proof_params.x * sigma;
  121|     68|
  122|     68|        // T_ρ = ρY
  123|     68|        let t_rho = proof_params.y * rho;
  124|     68|
  125|     68|        // δ_σ = yσ
  126|     68|        let delta_sigma = message * sigma;
  127|     68|
  128|     68|        // δ_ρ = yρ
  129|     68|        let delta_rho = message * rho;
  130|     68|
  131|     68|        // Randomly pick r_σ,r_ρ,r_δσ,r_δρ
  132|     68|        // r_y is either generated randomly or supplied in case this proof is used to
  133|     68|        // bind to an external proof
  134|     68|        let r_y = y.get_blinder(rng).unwrap();
  135|     68|        let r_sigma = generate_fr(SALT, None, rng);
  136|     68|        let r_rho = generate_fr(SALT, None, rng);
  137|     68|        let r_delta_sigma = generate_fr(SALT, None, rng);
  138|     68|        let r_delta_rho = generate_fr(SALT, None, rng);
  139|     68|
  140|     68|        // R_σ = r_σ X
  141|     68|        let cap_r_sigma = proof_params.x * r_sigma;
  142|     68|
  143|     68|        // R_ρ = ρY
  144|     68|        let cap_r_rho = proof_params.y * r_rho;
  145|     68|
  146|     68|        // R_δσ = r_y T_σ - r_δσ X
  147|     68|        let cap_r_delta_sigma = cap_r(&[t_sigma, -proof_params.x], &[r_y, r_delta_sigma]);
  148|     68|
  149|     68|        // R_δρ = r_y T_ρ - r_δρ Y
  150|     68|        let cap_r_delta_rho = cap_r(&[t_rho, -proof_params.y], &[r_y, r_delta_rho]);
  151|     68|
  152|     68|        // R_E = e(E_C, P~)^r_y
  153|     68|        let g2 = G2Projective::GENERATOR;
  154|     68|
  155|     68|        // R_E *= e(Z, P~)^-r_δσ - r_δρ
  156|     68|        let exp = -(r_delta_sigma + r_delta_rho);
  157|     68|
  158|     68|        // Optimize one less pairing by computing
  159|     68|        // R_E = e(E_C^r_y + Z^{-r_δσ - r_δρ}, P~)
  160|     68|        let mut lhs = e_c * r_y;
  161|     68|        let z = proof_params.z * exp;
  162|     68|        lhs += z;
  163|     68|        let mut cap_r_e = pair(lhs, g2);
  164|     68|
  165|     68|        // R_E *= e(Z, Q~)^-r_σ - r_ρ
  166|     68|        let exp = -(r_sigma + r_rho);
  167|     68|        cap_r_e += pairing(proof_params.z, pubkey.0, exp);
  168|     68|
  169|     68|        Self {
  170|     68|            e_c,
  171|     68|            t_sigma,
  172|     68|            t_rho,
  173|     68|            delta_sigma,
  174|     68|            delta_rho,
  175|     68|            blinding_factor: r_y,
  176|     68|            r_sigma,
  177|     68|            r_rho,
  178|     68|            r_delta_sigma,
  179|     68|            r_delta_rho,
  180|     68|            sigma,
  181|     68|            rho,
  182|     68|            cap_r_e,
  183|     68|            cap_r_sigma,
  184|     68|            cap_r_rho,
  185|     68|            cap_r_delta_sigma,
  186|     68|            cap_r_delta_rho,
  187|     68|            witness_value: message,
  188|     68|        }
  189|     68|    }

  256|       |    /// Generate the structure that can be used in the challenge hash
  257|       |    /// returns a struct to avoid recomputing
  258|    348|    pub fn finalize(
  259|    348|        &self,
  260|    348|        accumulator: Accumulator,
  261|    348|        proof_params: ProofParams,
  262|    348|        pubkey: PublicKey,
  263|    348|        challenge_hash: Element,
  264|    348|    ) -> MembershipProofFinal {
  265|    348|        let challenge_hash = challenge_hash.0;
  266|    348|        // R_σ = s_δ X - c T_σ
  267|    348|        let cap_r_sigma = cap_r(
  268|    348|            &[proof_params.x, -self.t_sigma],
  269|    348|            &[self.s_sigma, challenge_hash],
  270|    348|        );
  271|    348|
  272|    348|        // R_ρ = s_ρ Y - c T_ρ
  273|    348|        let cap_r_rho = cap_r(
  274|    348|            &[proof_params.y, -self.t_rho],
  275|    348|            &[self.s_rho, challenge_hash],
  276|    348|        );
  277|    348|
  278|    348|        // R_δσ =  s_y T_σ - s_δσ X
  279|    348|        let cap_r_delta_sigma = cap_r(
  280|    348|            &[self.t_sigma, -proof_params.x],
  281|    348|            &[self.s_y, self.s_delta_sigma],
  282|    348|        );
  283|    348|
  284|    348|        // R_δρ =  s_y T_ρ - s_δρ Y
  285|    348|        let cap_r_delta_rho = cap_r(
  286|    348|            &[self.t_rho, -proof_params.y],
  287|    348|            &[self.s_y, self.s_delta_rho],
  288|    348|        );
  289|    348|
  290|    348|        let g2 = G2Projective::GENERATOR;
  291|    348|
  292|    348|        // We can eliminate three pairings by combining
  293|    348|        // e(E_C, P~)^s_y * e(Z, P~)^-(s_delta_sigma + s_delta_rho) * e(V, P~)^-c
  294|    348|        // to
  295|    348|        // e(E_C^s_y + Z^-(s_delta_sigma + s_delta_rho) + V^-c, P~)
  296|    348|        // and
  297|    348|        // e(Z, Q~)^-(s_sigma + s_rho) * e(E_C, Q~)^c
  298|    348|        // to
  299|    348|        // e(Z^-(s_sigma + s_rho) + E_C^c, Q~)
  300|    348|
  301|    348|        // e(E_C, P~)^s_y
  302|    348|        let mut lhs = self.e_c * self.s_y;
  303|    348|
  304|    348|        // e(Z, P~)^-(s_delta_sigma + s_delta_rho)
  305|    348|        let mut exp = -(self.s_delta_sigma + self.s_delta_rho);
  306|    348|        let mut rhs = proof_params.z * exp;
  307|    348|        lhs += rhs;
  308|    348|
  309|    348|        // e(V, P~)^-c
  310|    348|        exp = -challenge_hash;
  311|    348|        rhs = accumulator.0 * exp;
  312|    348|        lhs += rhs;
  313|    348|        let mut cap_r_e = pair(lhs, g2);
  314|    348|
  315|    348|        // e(Z, Q~)^-(s_sigma + s_rho)
  316|    348|        exp = -(self.s_sigma + self.s_rho);
  317|    348|        lhs = proof_params.z * exp;
  318|    348|
  319|    348|        // e(E_C, Q~)^c
  320|    348|        rhs = self.e_c * challenge_hash;
  321|    348|        lhs += rhs;
  322|    348|        cap_r_e += pair(lhs, pubkey.0);
  323|    348|
  324|    348|        MembershipProofFinal {
  325|    348|            e_c: self.e_c,
  326|    348|            t_sigma: self.t_sigma,
  327|    348|            t_rho: self.t_rho,
  328|    348|            cap_r_e,
  329|    348|            cap_r_sigma,
  330|    348|            cap_r_rho,
  331|    348|            cap_r_delta_sigma,
  332|    348|            cap_r_delta_rho,
  333|    348|        }
  334|    348|    }
  335|       |

  347|      0|    }

  660|      0|        // e(E_C, P~)^s_y * e(Z, P~)^-(s_delta_sigma + s_delta_rho) * e(V, P~)^-c * e(K, P~)^-s_v * e(Ed, P~)^c

  691|      0|

  414|       |/// A compressed delta after evaluating the polynomials w.r.t an element
  415|       |#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, Eq, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
  416|       |pub struct Delta {
  417|       |    d: Scalar,

  421|       |/// Compress the deltas for the specified element and return the single delta
  422|      0|pub fn evaluate_deltas<A, D, C>(y: Element, deltas: &[(A, D, C)]) -> Result<Delta, Error>
  423|      0|where
  424|      0|    A: AsRef<[Element]>,
  425|      0|    D: AsRef<[Element]>,
  426|      0|    C: AsRef<[Coefficient]>,
  427|      0|{
  428|      0|    let one = Scalar::ONE;
  429|      0|
  430|      0|    // dA(x) =  ∏ 1..n (yA_i - x)
  431|      0|    let mut aa = Vec::new();
  432|      0|    // dD(x) = ∏ 1..m (yD_i - x)
  433|      0|    let mut dd = Vec::new();
  434|      0|
  435|      0|    let mut acc_a = one;
  436|      0|    let mut acc_d = one;
  437|       |
  438|       |    // dA = ∏ a..b dA
  439|       |    // dD = ∏ a..b dD
  440|      0|    for (adds, dels, _) in deltas {
  441|      0|        let ta = dad(adds.as_ref(), y.0);
  442|      0|        let td = dad(dels.as_ref(), y.0);
  443|      0|
  444|      0|        acc_a *= ta;
  445|      0|        acc_d *= td;
  446|      0|
  447|      0|        aa.push(ta);
  448|      0|        dd.push(td);
  449|      0|    }
  450|       |
  451|      0|    let tv = acc_d.invert();
  452|      0|    // If this fails, then this value was removed
  453|      0|    if tv.is_none().unwrap_u8() == 1 {
  454|      0|        return Err(Error::from_msg(1, "no inverse exists"));
  455|      0|    }
  456|      0|    acc_d = tv.unwrap();
  457|      0|
  458|      0|    //〈Υy,Ω〉
  459|      0|    let mut poly = PolynomialG1::with_capacity(deltas.len());
  460|       |
  461|       |    // Ωi->j+1 = ∑ 1..t (dAt * dDt-1) · Ω
  462|      0|    for i in 0..deltas.len() {
  463|       |        // t = i+1
  464|       |        // ∏^(t-1)_(h=i+1)
  465|      0|        let mut ddh = one;
  466|      0|        for h in dd.iter().take(i) {
  467|      0|            ddh *= h;
  468|      0|        }
  469|       |
  470|      0|        let mut dak = one;
  471|       |        // ∏^(j+1)_(k=t+1)
  472|      0|        for k in aa.iter().take(deltas.len()).skip(i + 1) {
  473|      0|            dak *= k;
  474|      0|        }
  475|       |
  476|      0|        dak *= ddh;
  477|      0|        let mut pp = PolynomialG1(deltas[i].2.as_ref().iter().map(|c| c.0).collect());
  478|      0|        pp *= dak;
  479|      0|        poly += pp;
  480|      0|    }
  481|       |
  482|      0|    acc_a *= acc_d;
  483|       |
  484|      0|    if let Some(mut v) = poly.evaluate(y.0) {
  485|       |        // 1 / dD *〈Υy,Ω〉
  486|      0|        v *= acc_d;
  487|      0|        Ok(Delta { d: acc_a, p: v })
  488|       |    } else {
  489|      0|        Err(Error::from_msg(2, "polynomial could not be evaluated"))
  490|       |    }
  491|      0|}

  493|       |/// Computes the compressed delta needed to update a witness
  494|      0|pub fn evaluate_delta<A, D, C>(
  495|      0|    y: Element,
  496|      0|    additions: A,
  497|      0|    deletions: D,
  498|      0|    coefficients: C,
  499|      0|) -> Result<Delta, Error>
  500|      0|where
  501|      0|    A: AsRef<[Element]>,
  502|      0|    D: AsRef<[Element]>,
  503|      0|    C: AsRef<[Coefficient]>,
  504|      0|{
  505|      0|    // dD(x) = ∏ 1..m (yD_i - x)
  506|      0|    let mut d_d = dad(deletions.as_ref(), y.0);
  507|      0|
  508|      0|    let t = d_d.invert();
  509|      0|    // If this fails, then this value was removed
  510|      0|    if t.is_none().unwrap_u8() == 1 {
  511|      0|        return Err(Error::from_msg(1, "no inverse exists"));
  512|      0|    }
  513|      0|    d_d = t.unwrap();
  514|      0|
  515|      0|    //dA(x) =  ∏ 1..n (yA_i - x)
  516|      0|    let mut d_a = dad(additions.as_ref(), y.0);
  517|      0|    d_a *= d_d;
  518|      0|
  519|      0|    let poly = PolynomialG1(
  520|      0|        coefficients
  521|      0|            .as_ref()
  522|      0|            .iter()
  523|      0|            .map(|c| c.0)
  524|      0|            .collect::<Vec<G1Projective>>(),
  525|      0|    );
  526|       |    //〈Υy,Ω〉
  527|      0|    if let Some(mut v) = poly.evaluate(y.0) {
  528|       |        // C + 1 / dD *〈Υy,Ω〉
  529|      0|        v *= d_d;
  530|      0|        Ok(Delta { d: d_a, p: v })
  531|       |    } else {
  532|      0|        Err(Error::from_msg(2, "polynomial could not be evaluated"))
  533|       |    }

   27|       |    /// Assumes the proof of hidden messages
   28|       |    /// If other proofs were included, those will need to be verified another way
   29|     81|    fn verify(
   30|     81|        &self,
   31|     81|        known_messages: &[usize],
   32|     81|        sk: &Self::SecretKey,
   33|     81|        nonce: Scalar,

   36|     81|        let mut known = BTreeSet::new();

   28|     68|        public_key: &Self::PublicKey,

   50|      0|                    hidden_messages.push(*msg);

   54|    170|                    hidden_messages.push(*msg);

    3|       |use crate::CredxResult;
    4|       |use blsful::inner_types::{G1Projective, Scalar};
    5|       |use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
    6|       |use sha3::digest::{ExtendableOutput, Update, XofReader};
    7|       |use subtle::CtOption;
    8|       |
    9|       |/// A PS blind signature
   10|       |/// structurally identical to `Signature` but is used to
   11|       |/// help with misuse and confusion.
   12|       |///
   13|       |/// 1 or more messages have been hidden by the signature recipient
   14|       |/// so the signer only knows a subset of the messages to be signed
   15|       |#[derive(Debug, Default, Copy, Clone, Eq, PartialEq, Deserialize, Serialize)]
   16|       |pub struct BlindSignature(pub(crate) Signature);
   17|       |
   18|       |impl BlindSignatureTrait for BlindSignature {
   19|       |    type SecretKey = SecretKey;
   20|       |    type PublicKey = PublicKey;
   21|       |    type Signature = Signature;
   22|       |
   23|      0|    fn new(
   24|      0|        commitment: G1Projective,
   25|      0|        sk: &SecretKey,
   26|      0|        msgs: &[(usize, Scalar)],
   27|      0|    ) -> CredxResult<Self> {
   28|      0|        Self::new(commitment, sk, msgs)
   29|      0|    }
   30|       |
   31|      0|    fn to_unblinded(self, blinding: Scalar) -> Signature {
   32|      0|        self.to_unblinded(blinding)
   33|      0|    }
   34|       |}
   35|       |
   36|       |impl BlindSignature {
   37|       |    /// The size of the signature in bytes
   38|       |    pub const BYTES: usize = 128;
   39|       |
   40|       |    /// Generate a new signature where all messages are known to the signer
   41|      0|    pub fn new(
   42|      0|        commitment: G1Projective,
   43|      0|        sk: &SecretKey,
   44|      0|        msgs: &[(usize, Scalar)],
   45|      0|    ) -> CredxResult<Self> {
   46|      0|        if sk.y.len() < msgs.len() {
   47|      0|            return Err(crate::error::Error::InvalidSigningOperation);
   48|      0|        }
   49|      0|        if sk.is_invalid() {
   50|      0|            return Err(crate::error::Error::InvalidSigningOperation);
   51|      0|        }
   52|      0|
   53|      0|        let t_msgs = msgs.iter().map(|(_, m)| *m).collect::<Vec<Scalar>>();
   54|      0|        let m_tick = Signature::compute_m_tick(t_msgs.as_slice());
   55|      0|
   56|      0|        let mut hasher = sha3::Shake256::default();
   57|      0|        hasher.update(&sk.to_bytes());
   58|      0|        t_msgs.iter().for_each(|m| hasher.update(&m.to_be_bytes()));
   59|      0|
   60|      0|        let mut reader = hasher.finalize_xof();
   61|      0|        let mut okm = [0u8; 64];
   62|      0|        reader.read(&mut okm);
   63|      0|
   64|      0|        // Should yield non-zero values for `u` and m', very small likelihood of it being zero
   65|      0|        let u = Scalar::from_bytes_wide(&okm);
   66|      0|        let sigma_1 = G1Projective::GENERATOR * u;
   67|      0|
   68|      0|        let mut exp = sk.x + m_tick * sk.w;
   69|      0|        for (i, msg) in msgs {
   70|      0|            exp += sk.y[*i] * msg;
   71|      0|        }
   72|      0|        let mut sigma_2 = (G1Projective::GENERATOR * exp) + commitment;
   73|      0|        sigma_2 *= u;
   74|      0|        Ok(Self(Signature {
   75|      0|            sigma_1,
   76|      0|            sigma_2,
   77|      0|            m_tick,
   78|      0|        }))
   79|      0|    }
   80|       |
   81|       |    /// Once signature on committed attributes (blind signature) is received, the signature needs to be unblinded.
   82|       |    /// Takes the blinding factor used in the commitment.
   83|      0|    pub fn to_unblinded(self, blinding: Scalar) -> Signature {
   84|      0|        Signature {
   85|      0|            sigma_1: self.0.sigma_1,
   86|      0|            sigma_2: self.0.sigma_2 - (self.0.sigma_1 * blinding),
   87|      0|            m_tick: self.0.m_tick,
   88|      0|        }
   89|      0|    }
   90|       |
   91|       |    /// Get the byte representation of this signature
   92|      0|    pub fn to_bytes(&self) -> [u8; Self::BYTES] {
   93|      0|        self.0.to_bytes()
   94|      0|    }
   95|       |
   96|       |    /// Convert a byte sequence into a signature
   97|      0|    pub fn from_bytes(data: &[u8; Self::BYTES]) -> CtOption<Self> {
   98|      0|        Signature::from_bytes(data).map(Self)
   99|      0|    }
  100|       |}

   12|       |/// Contains the data used for computing a blind signature and verifying
   13|       |/// proof of hidden messages from a prover
   14|       |#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   15|       |pub struct BlindSignatureContext {
   16|       |    /// The blinded signature commitment
   17|       |    pub commitment: G1Projective,
   18|       |    /// The challenge hash for the Fiat-Shamir heuristic
   19|       |    pub challenge: Scalar,
   20|       |    /// The proofs for the hidden messages
   21|       |    pub proofs: Vec<Scalar>,
   22|       |}
   23|       |
   24|       |impl BlindSignatureContext {
   25|       |    /// Store the generators as a sequence of bytes
   26|       |    /// Each point is compressed to big-endian format
   27|       |    /// Needs (N + 1) * 32 + 48 * 2 space otherwise it will panic
   28|       |    pub fn to_bytes(&self) -> Vec<u8> {
   29|       |        let mut buffer = Vec::new();
   30|       |        buffer.extend_from_slice(self.commitment.to_affine().to_compressed().as_ref());
   31|       |        buffer.extend_from_slice(&self.challenge.to_be_bytes());
   32|       |
   33|       |        for i in 0..self.proofs.len() {
   34|       |            buffer.extend_from_slice(&self.proofs[i].to_be_bytes());
   35|       |        }
   36|       |        buffer
   37|       |    }
   38|       |
   39|       |    /// Convert a byte sequence into the blind signature context
   40|       |    /// Expected size is (N + 1) * 32 + 48 bytes
   41|      0|    pub fn from_bytes<B: AsRef<[u8]>>(bytes: B) -> Option<Self> {
   42|      0|        let size = 32 * 2 + 48;
   43|      0|        let buffer = bytes.as_ref();
   44|      0|        if buffer.len() < size {
   45|      0|            return None;
   46|      0|        }
   47|      0|        if buffer.len() - 48 % 32 != 0 {
   48|      0|            return None;
   49|      0|        }
   50|      0|
   51|      0|        let commitment = G1Affine::from_compressed(&<[u8; 48]>::try_from(&buffer[..48]).unwrap())
   52|      0|            .map(G1Projective::from);
   53|      0|        if commitment.is_none().unwrap_u8() == 1 {
   54|      0|            return None;
   55|      0|        }
   56|      0|        let mut offset = 48;
   57|      0|        let mut end = 80;
   58|      0|
   59|      0|        let challenge = Scalar::from_be_bytes(&<[u8; 32]>::try_from(&buffer[offset..end]).unwrap());
   60|      0|        if challenge.is_none().unwrap_u8() == 1 {
   61|      0|            return None;
   62|      0|        }
   63|      0|
   64|      0|        let times = (buffer.len() - 48 - 32) / 32;
   65|      0|
   66|      0|        offset = end;
   67|      0|        end += 32;
   68|      0|
   69|      0|        let mut proofs = Vec::new();
   70|      0|        for _ in 0..times {
   71|      0|            let p = Scalar::from_be_bytes(&<[u8; 32]>::try_from(&buffer[offset..end]).unwrap());
   72|      0|            if p.is_none().unwrap_u8() == 1 {
   73|      0|                return None;
   74|      0|            }
   75|      0|            proofs.push(p.unwrap());
   76|      0|            offset = end;
   77|      0|            end += 32;
   78|       |        }
   79|       |
   80|      0|        Some(Self {
   81|      0|            commitment: commitment.unwrap(),
   82|      0|            challenge: challenge.unwrap(),
   83|      0|            proofs,
   84|      0|        })
   85|      0|    }
   86|       |}
   87|       |
   88|       |impl BlindSignatureContextTrait for BlindSignatureContext {
   89|       |    type SecretKey = SecretKey;
   90|       |
   91|       |    /// Assumes the proof of hidden messages
   92|       |    /// If other proofs were included, those will need to be verified another way
   93|       |    fn verify(
   94|       |        &self,
   95|       |        known_messages: &[usize],
   96|       |        sk: &Self::SecretKey,
   97|       |        nonce: Scalar,
   98|       |    ) -> CredxResult<bool> {
   99|       |        let mut known = BTreeSet::new();
  100|       |        let mut points = Vec::new();
  101|       |        for idx in known_messages {
  102|       |            if *idx >= sk.y.len() {
  103|       |                return Err(crate::error::Error::InvalidSignatureProofData);
  104|       |            }
  105|       |            known.insert(*idx);
  106|       |        }
  107|       |        for i in 0..sk.y.len() {
  108|       |            if !known.contains(&i) {
  109|       |                points.push(G1Projective::GENERATOR * sk.y[i]);
  110|       |            }
  111|       |        }
  112|       |        points.push(G1Projective::GENERATOR);
  113|       |        points.push(self.commitment);
  114|       |
  115|       |        let mut scalars = self.proofs.clone();
  116|       |        scalars.push(-self.challenge);
  117|       |
  118|       |        let mut transcript = Transcript::new(b"new blind signature");
  119|       |        let pk = PublicKey::from(sk);
  120|       |        transcript.append_message(b"public key", pk.to_bytes().as_ref());
  121|       |        let mut res = [0u8; 64];
  122|       |
  123|       |        let commitment = G1Projective::sum_of_products(points.as_ref(), scalars.as_ref());
  124|       |        transcript.append_message(
  125|       |            b"random commitment",
  126|       |            &commitment.to_affine().to_compressed(),
  127|       |        );
  128|       |        transcript.append_message(
  129|       |            b"blind commitment",
  130|       |            &self.commitment.to_affine().to_compressed(),
  131|       |        );
  132|       |        transcript.append_message(b"nonce", &nonce.to_be_bytes());
  133|       |        transcript.challenge_bytes(b"blind signature context challenge", &mut res);
  134|       |        let challenge = Scalar::from_bytes_wide(&res);
  135|       |
  136|       |        Ok(self.challenge.ct_eq(&challenge).unwrap_u8() == 1)
  137|       |    }
  138|       |}

   11|       |/// Proof of Knowledge of a Signature that is used by the prover

   13|       |pub struct PokSignature {
   14|       |    secrets: Vec<Scalar>,
   15|       |    proof: ProofCommittedBuilder<G2Projective, G2Affine, Scalar>,

   21|       |impl ProofOfSignatureKnowledgeContribution for PokSignature {
   22|       |    type Signature = Signature;
   23|       |    type PublicKey = PublicKey;
   24|       |    type ProofOfKnowledge = PokSignatureProof;
   25|       |
   26|       |    /// Creates the initial proof data before a Fiat-Shamir calculation
   27|      0|    fn commit(
   28|      0|        signature: &Signature,
   29|      0|        public_key: &PublicKey,
   30|      0|        messages: &[ProofMessage<Scalar>],
   31|      0|        mut rng: impl RngCore + CryptoRng,
   32|      0|    ) -> CredxResult<Self> {
   33|      0|        if public_key.y.len() < messages.len() {
   34|      0|            return Err(crate::error::Error::General("ProofCommitmentError"));
   35|      0|        }
   36|      0|
   37|      0|        let r = Scalar::random(&mut rng);
   38|      0|        let t = Scalar::random(&mut rng);
   39|      0|
   40|      0|        // ZKP for signature
   41|      0|        let sigma_1 = signature.sigma_1 * r;
   42|      0|        let sigma_2 = (signature.sigma_2 + (signature.sigma_1 * t)) * r;
   43|      0|
   44|      0|        // Prove knowledge of m_tick, m_1, m_2, ... for all hidden m_i and t in J = Y_tilde_1^m_1 * Y_tilde_2^m_2 * ..... * g_tilde^t
   45|      0|        let mut proof = ProofCommittedBuilder::new(G2Projective::sum_of_products);
   46|      0|        let mut points = Vec::new();
   47|      0|        let mut secrets = Vec::new();
   48|      0|
   49|      0|        proof.commit_random(G2Projective::GENERATOR, &mut rng);
   50|      0|        points.push(G2Projective::GENERATOR);
   51|      0|        secrets.push(t);
   52|      0|
   53|      0|        proof.commit_random(public_key.w, &mut rng);
   54|      0|        points.push(public_key.w);
   55|      0|        secrets.push(signature.m_tick);
   56|       |
   57|      0|        for (i, m) in messages.iter().enumerate() {
   58|      0|            match m {
   59|      0|                ProofMessage::Hidden(HiddenMessage::ProofSpecificBlinding(msg)) => {
   60|      0|                    proof.commit_random(public_key.y[i], &mut rng);
   61|      0|                    points.push(public_key.y[i]);
   62|      0|                    secrets.push(*msg);
   63|      0|                }
   64|      0|                ProofMessage::Hidden(HiddenMessage::ExternalBlinding(msg, n)) => {
   65|      0|                    proof.commit(public_key.y[i], *n);
   66|      0|                    points.push(public_key.y[i]);
   67|      0|                    secrets.push(*msg);
   68|      0|                }
   69|      0|                ProofMessage::Revealed(_) => {}
   70|       |            }
   71|       |        }
   72|      0|        let commitment = G2Projective::sum_of_products(points.as_ref(), secrets.as_ref());
   73|      0|        Ok(Self {
   74|      0|            secrets,
   75|      0|            proof,
   76|      0|            commitment,
   77|      0|            sigma_1,
   78|      0|            sigma_2,
   79|      0|        })
   80|      0|    }
   81|       |
   82|       |    /// Convert the committed values to bytes for the fiat-shamir challenge
   83|       |    fn add_proof_contribution(&self, transcript: &mut Transcript) {
   84|       |        transcript.append_message(
   85|       |            b"sigma_1",
   86|       |            self.sigma_1.to_affine().to_compressed().as_ref(),
   87|       |        );
   88|       |        transcript.append_message(
   89|       |            b"sigma_2",
   90|       |            self.sigma_2.to_affine().to_compressed().as_ref(),
   91|       |        );
   92|       |        transcript.append_message(
   93|       |            b"random commitment",
   94|       |            self.commitment.to_affine().to_compressed().as_ref(),
   95|       |        );
   96|       |        self.proof
   97|       |            .add_challenge_contribution(b"blind commitment", transcript);
   98|       |    }
   99|       |
  100|       |    /// Generate the Schnorr challenges for the selective disclosure proofs
  101|       |    fn generate_proof(self, challenge: Scalar) -> CredxResult<PokSignatureProof> {
  102|       |        let proof = self
  103|       |            .proof
  104|       |            .generate_proof(challenge, self.secrets.as_ref())?;
  105|       |        Ok(PokSignatureProof {
  106|       |            sigma_1: self.sigma_1,

    1|       |use super::PublicKey;
    2|       |use crate::error::Error;
    3|       |use crate::knox::short_group_sig_core::short_group_traits::ProofOfSignatureKnowledge;
    4|       |use crate::CredxResult;
    5|       |use blsful::inner_types::*;
    6|       |use core::ops::BitOr;
    7|       |use elliptic_curve::group::prime::PrimeCurveAffine;
    8|       |use merlin::Transcript;
    9|       |use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
   10|       |use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
   11|       |
   12|       |/// The actual proof that is sent from prover to verifier.
   13|       |#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize, Serialize)]
   14|       |pub struct PokSignatureProof {
   15|       |    pub(crate) sigma_1: G1Projective,
   16|       |    pub(crate) sigma_2: G1Projective,
   17|       |    pub(crate) commitment: G2Projective,
   18|       |    pub(crate) proof: Vec<Scalar>,
   19|       |}
   20|       |
   21|       |impl ProofOfSignatureKnowledge for PokSignatureProof {
   22|       |    type PublicKey = PublicKey;
   23|       |
   24|       |    /// Convert the committed values to bytes for the fiat-shamir challenge
   25|       |    fn add_proof_contribution(
   26|       |        &self,
   27|       |        public_key: &PublicKey,
   28|       |        rvl_msgs: &[(usize, Scalar)],
   29|       |        challenge: Scalar,
   30|       |        transcript: &mut Transcript,
   31|       |    ) {
   32|       |        transcript.append_message(
   33|       |            b"sigma_1",
   34|       |            self.sigma_1.to_affine().to_compressed().as_ref(),
   35|       |        );
   36|       |        transcript.append_message(
   37|       |            b"sigma_2",
   38|       |            self.sigma_2.to_affine().to_compressed().as_ref(),
   39|       |        );
   40|       |        transcript.append_message(
   41|       |            b"random commitment",
   42|       |            self.commitment.to_affine().to_compressed().as_ref(),
   43|       |        );
   44|       |
   45|       |        let mut points = Vec::new();
   46|       |
   47|       |        points.push(G2Projective::GENERATOR);
   48|       |        points.push(public_key.w);
   49|       |
   50|       |        let mut known = BTreeSet::new();
   51|       |        for (idx, _) in rvl_msgs {
   52|       |            known.insert(*idx);
   53|       |        }
   54|       |
   55|       |        for i in 0..public_key.y.len() {
   56|       |            if known.contains(&i) {
   57|       |                continue;
   58|       |            }
   59|       |            points.push(public_key.y[i]);
   60|       |        }
   61|       |        points.push(self.commitment);
   62|       |
   63|       |        let mut scalars = self.proof.clone();
   64|       |        scalars.push(-challenge);
   65|       |        let commitment = G2Projective::sum_of_products(points.as_ref(), scalars.as_ref());
   66|       |        transcript.append_message(
   67|       |            b"blind commitment",
   68|       |            commitment.to_affine().to_compressed().as_ref(),
   69|       |        );
   70|       |    }
   71|       |
   72|       |    /// Validate the proof, only checks the signature proof
   73|       |    /// the selective disclosure proof is checked by verifying
   74|       |    /// self.challenge == computed_challenge
   75|       |    fn verify(
   76|       |        &self,
   77|       |        public_key: &Self::PublicKey,
   78|       |        revealed_messages: &[(usize, Scalar)],
   79|       |        _challenge: Scalar,
   80|       |    ) -> CredxResult<()> {
   81|       |        // check the signature proof
   82|       |        if self
   83|       |            .sigma_1
   84|       |            .is_identity()
   85|       |            .bitor(self.sigma_2.is_identity())
   86|       |            .unwrap_u8()
   87|       |            == 1
   88|       |        {
   89|       |            return Err(Error::General("Invalid proof - identity"));
   90|       |        }
   91|       |
   92|       |        if public_key.y.len() < revealed_messages.len() {
   93|       |            return Err(Error::General(
   94|       |                "Invalid key - revealed messages length is bigger than the public key",
   95|       |            ));
   96|       |        }
   97|       |        if public_key.is_invalid().into() {
   98|       |            return Err(Error::General("Invalid public key"));
   99|       |        }
  100|       |
  101|       |        let mut points = Vec::new();
  102|       |        let mut scalars = Vec::new();
  103|       |
  104|       |        for (idx, msg) in revealed_messages {
  105|       |            if *idx > public_key.y.len() {
  106|       |                return Err(Error::General("Invalid proof - revealed message index"));
  107|       |            }
  108|       |            points.push(public_key.y[*idx]);
  109|       |            scalars.push(*msg);
  110|       |        }
  111|       |        points.push(public_key.x);
  112|       |        scalars.push(Scalar::ONE);
  113|       |        points.push(self.commitment);
  114|       |        scalars.push(Scalar::ONE);
  115|       |
  116|       |        let j = G2Projective::sum_of_products(points.as_ref(), scalars.as_ref());
  117|       |
  118|       |        let res = multi_miller_loop(&[
  119|       |            (&self.sigma_1.to_affine(), &G2Prepared::from(j.to_affine())),
  120|       |            (
  121|       |                &self.sigma_2.to_affine(),
  122|       |                &G2Prepared::from(-G2Affine::generator()),
  123|       |            ),
  124|       |        ])
  125|       |        .final_exponentiation()
  126|       |        .is_identity()
  127|       |        .unwrap_u8()
  128|       |            == 1;
  129|       |        if res {
  130|       |            Ok(())
  131|       |        } else {
  132|       |            Err(Error::General("Invalid proof - signature proof"))
  133|       |        }
  134|       |    }
  135|       |
  136|       |    /// Return the Schnorr proofs for all hidden messages
  137|       |    fn get_hidden_message_proofs(
  138|       |        &self,
  139|       |        public_key: &PublicKey,
  140|       |        rvl_msgs: &[(usize, Scalar)],
  141|       |    ) -> CredxResult<BTreeMap<usize, Scalar>> {
  142|       |        if public_key.y.len() < rvl_msgs.len() {
  143|       |            return Err(Error::General("Proof error"));
  144|       |        }
  145|       |        if public_key.is_invalid().unwrap_u8() == 1u8 {
  146|       |            return Err(Error::General("Proof error"));
  147|       |        }
  148|       |        let mut hidden = BTreeMap::new();
  149|       |        let mut j = 0;
  150|       |        for i in 0..public_key.y.len() {
  151|       |            if j < rvl_msgs.len() && rvl_msgs[j].0 == i {
  152|       |                j += 1;
  153|       |                continue;
  154|       |            }
  155|       |            let message = self
  156|       |                .proof
  157|       |                // + 2 because ProofOfSignatureKnowledgeContribution.commit adds
  158|       |                // two secrets before adding secrets for attributes
  159|       |                .get(i + 2 - j)
  160|       |                .ok_or(Error::General("invalid proof"))?;
  161|       |            hidden.insert(i, *message);
  162|       |        }
  163|       |
  164|       |        Ok(hidden)
  165|       |    }
  166|       |}
  167|       |
  168|       |impl PokSignatureProof {
  169|       |    /// Store the proof as a sequence of bytes
  170|       |    /// Each point is compressed to big-endian format
  171|       |    /// Needs (N + 2) * 32 + 48 * 2 + 96 space otherwise it will panic
  172|       |    /// where N is the number of hidden messages
  173|       |    pub fn to_bytes(&self) -> Vec<u8> {
  174|       |        let mut buffer = Vec::with_capacity(48 * 2 + 96 + 32 * self.proof.len());
  175|       |        buffer.extend_from_slice(&self.sigma_1.to_affine().to_compressed());
  176|       |        buffer.extend_from_slice(&self.sigma_2.to_affine().to_compressed());
  177|       |        buffer.extend_from_slice(&self.commitment.to_affine().to_compressed());
  178|       |
  179|       |        for m in &self.proof {
  180|       |            buffer.extend_from_slice(m.to_be_bytes().as_ref());
  181|       |        }
  182|       |        buffer
  183|       |    }
  184|       |
  185|       |    /// Convert a byte sequence into a Signature Proof of Knowledge
  186|      0|    pub fn from_bytes<B: AsRef<[u8]>>(bytes: B) -> Option<Self> {
  187|       |        const SIZE: usize = 32 * 3 + 48 * 4;
  188|      0|        let buffer = bytes.as_ref();
  189|      0|        if buffer.len() < SIZE {
  190|      0|            return None;
  191|      0|        }
  192|      0|        if buffer.len() % 32 != 0 {
  193|      0|            return None;
  194|      0|        }
  195|      0|
  196|      0|        let hid_msg_cnt = (buffer.len() - 48 * 4) / 32;
  197|      0|        let mut offset = 48;
  198|      0|        let mut end = 96;
  199|      0|        let sigma_1 = G1Affine::from_compressed(&<[u8; 48]>::try_from(&buffer[..offset]).unwrap())

  202|      0|            G1Affine::from_compressed(&<[u8; 48]>::try_from(&buffer[offset..end]).unwrap())

  205|      0|        end += 96;

  207|      0|            G2Affine::from_compressed(&<[u8; 96]>::try_from(&buffer[offset..end]).unwrap())

  209|      0|
  210|      0|        if sigma_1.is_none().unwrap_u8() == 1
  211|      0|            || sigma_2.is_none().unwrap_u8() == 1
  212|      0|            || commitment.is_none().unwrap_u8() == 1
  213|       |        {
  214|      0|            return None;
  215|      0|        }
  216|      0|
  217|      0|        offset = end;
  218|      0|        end += 32;
  219|      0|
  220|      0|        let mut proof = Vec::new();
  221|      0|        for _ in 0..hid_msg_cnt {
  222|      0|            let c = Scalar::from_be_bytes(&<[u8; 32]>::try_from(&buffer[offset..end]).unwrap());
  223|      0|            offset = end;
  224|      0|            end = offset + 32;

  226|      0|                return None;
  227|      0|            }
  228|      0|
  229|      0|            proof.push(c.unwrap());
  230|       |        }
  231|      0|        Some(Self {
  232|      0|            sigma_1: sigma_1.unwrap(),
  233|      0|            sigma_2: sigma_2.unwrap(),

  246|       |                }

  471|      0|fn decode_credential_from_w3c(
  472|      0|    w3c_presentation: &Value,
  473|      0|) -> CredxResult<(Map<String, Value>, Map<String, Value>)> {
  474|      0|    let credential = &w3c_presentation["verifiableCredential"];
  475|      0|    let credential_list = credential.as_array().unwrap();
  476|      0|    let mut disclosed_msg = Map::new();
  477|      0|    let mut combined_proofs = Map::new();
  478|       |
  479|      0|    for c in credential_list {
  480|      0|        let credential_proof = &c["proof"]["proofValue"];
  481|      0|        let decode_proof: (String, HashMap<String, Value>) =
  482|      0|            decode_proofs(credential_proof.as_str().unwrap()).unwrap();
  483|      0|        combined_proofs.extend(decode_proof.1.clone());
  484|      0|
  485|      0|        let mut signature_id = String::new();
  486|      0|        for (key, value) in decode_proof.1 {
  487|      0|            if let Some(inner_map) = value.as_object() {
  488|      0|                if inner_map.contains_key("Signature") {
  489|      0|                    signature_id = key.clone();
  490|      0|                    break;
  491|      0|                }
  492|      0|            }
  493|       |        }
  494|       |
  495|      0|        let credential_subject = &c["credentialSubject"];
  496|      0|        let credential_subject_map = credential_subject.as_object().unwrap();
  497|      0|
  498|      0|        let mut msg = HashMap::new();
  499|      0|        for (k, v) in credential_subject_map {
  500|      0|            if let Some(inner_map) = v.as_object() {
  501|      0|                if let Some(Value::String(inner_value)) = inner_map.get("value") {
  502|      0|                    msg.insert(k.clone(), inner_value.clone());
  503|      0|                }
  504|      0|            }
  505|       |        }
  506|      0|        disclosed_msg.insert(signature_id, to_value(&msg).unwrap());
  507|       |    }
  508|      0|    Ok((disclosed_msg, combined_proofs))

  521|      0|                                inner_key,

  524|      0|                                        "value": inner_value,
  525|      0|                                        "print_friendly": true
  526|      0|                                    }
  527|      0|                                }
  528|      0|                            ])
  529|      0|                        })
  530|      0|                        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
  531|      0|                })
  532|      0|            ])
  533|      0|        })

   11|       |/// A commitment builder
   12|       |#[derive(Debug)]
   13|       |pub(crate) struct CommitmentBuilder<'a> {
   14|       |    pub(crate) commitment: G1Projective,
   15|       |    pub(crate) statement: &'a CommitmentStatement<G1Projective>,
   16|       |    /// Independent Pedersen commitment blinder retained by the holder.
   17|       |    pub(crate) commitment_blinder: Scalar,
   18|       |    /// Independent Schnorr randomizer for the commitment blinder response.
   19|       |    pub(crate) blinder_randomizer: Scalar,
   20|       |}
   21|       |
   22|       |impl<S: ShortGroupSignatureScheme> PresentationBuilder<S> for CommitmentBuilder<'_> {
   23|     64|    fn gen_proof(self, challenge: Scalar) -> PresentationProofs<S> {
   24|     64|        let blinder_proof = self.blinder_randomizer + challenge * self.commitment_blinder;
   25|     64|        CommitmentProof {
   26|     64|            id: self.statement.id.clone(),
   27|     64|            commitment: self.commitment,
   28|     64|            blinder_proof,
   29|     64|        }
   30|     64|        .into()
   31|     64|    }
   32|       |}
   33|       |
   34|       |impl<'a> CommitmentBuilder<'a> {
   35|       |    /// Creates a commitment builder
   36|     66|    pub fn commit(
   37|     66|        statement: &'a CommitmentStatement<G1Projective>,
   38|     66|        message: Scalar,
   39|     66|        message_randomizer: Scalar,
   40|     66|        commitment_blinder: Scalar,
   41|     66|        mut rng: impl RngCore + CryptoRng,
   42|     66|        transcript: &mut Transcript,
   43|     66|    ) -> CredxResult<Self> {
   44|     66|        let blinder_randomizer = Scalar::random(&mut rng);
   45|     66|        let commitment = statement.message_generator * message
   46|     66|            + statement.blinder_generator * commitment_blinder;
   47|     66|        let blind_commitment = statement.message_generator * message_randomizer
   48|     66|            + statement.blinder_generator * blinder_randomizer;
   49|     66|
   50|     66|        transcript.append_message(b"", statement.id.as_bytes());
   51|     66|        transcript.append_message(
   52|     66|            b"commitment",
   53|     66|            commitment.to_affine().to_compressed().as_slice(),
   54|     66|        );
   55|     66|        transcript.append_message(
   56|     66|            b"blind commitment",
   57|     66|            blind_commitment.to_affine().to_compressed().as_slice(),
   58|     66|        );
   59|     66|        Ok(Self {
   60|     66|            commitment,
   61|     66|            statement,
   62|     66|            commitment_blinder,
   63|     66|            blinder_randomizer,
   64|     66|        })
   65|     66|    }

   15|       |
   16|       |    /// Create a presentation while allowing the holder to supply independent
   17|       |    /// Pedersen blinders for selected commitment statement IDs.  The map is
   18|       |    /// holder-local secret input and is never serialized into the proof.
   19|       |    ///
   20|       |    /// This separates the Pedersen blinder from the hidden BBS message's
   21|       |    /// Schnorr randomizer.  Reusing that randomizer as the commitment blinder
   22|       |    /// permits offline enumeration of low-entropy hidden attributes.
   23|     34|    pub fn create_with_commitment_blinders(
   24|     34|        credentials: &IndexMap<String, PresentationCredential<S>>,
   25|     34|        schema: &PresentationSchema<S>,
   26|     34|        nonce: &[u8],
   27|     34|        commitment_blinders: &IndexMap<String, Scalar>,
   28|     34|    ) -> CredxResult<Self> {
   29|     34|        let rng = OsRng {};
   30|     34|        let mut transcript = Transcript::new(b"credx presentation");
   31|     34|        Self::add_curve_parameters_challenge_contribution(&mut transcript);
   32|     34|        transcript.append_message(b"nonce", nonce);
   33|     34|        schema.add_challenge_contribution(&mut transcript);
   34|     34|
   35|     34|        let (signature_statements, predicate_statements) = Self::split_statements(schema);

  222|       |impl VerifiableEncryptionDecryptionProof {
  223|       |    pub fn decrypt_and_verify(
  224|       |        &self,
  225|       |        decryption_key: &SecretKey<Bls12381G2Impl>,
  226|       |    ) -> CredxResult<ClaimData> {
  227|       |        // 12 for the nonce
  228|       |        // 16 for the tag
  229|       |        if self.ciphertext.len() < 12 + 16 {
  230|       |            return Err(Error::General("arbitrary data ciphertext is too short"));
  231|       |        }
  232|       |
  233|       |        let input = self.c1 * decryption_key.0;
  234|       |        let expected_commitment = self.c2 - input;
  235|       |        let nonce = Nonce::from_slice(&self.ciphertext[..12]);
  236|       |
  237|       |        let mut aes_transcript =
  238|       |            Transcript::new(b"PresentationEncryptionDecryption arbitrary data derive aes key");
  239|       |        aes_transcript.append_message(b"key ikm", input.to_compressed().as_slice());
  240|       |        let mut okm = [0u8; 32];
  241|       |        aes_transcript.challenge_bytes(b"aes key", &mut okm);
  242|       |
  243|       |        let key = aes_gcm::Key::<Aes128Gcm>::from_slice(&okm[..16]);
  244|       |        let aad = okm[16..]
  245|       |            .iter()
  246|       |            .copied()
  247|       |            .chain(self.c1.to_compressed())
  248|       |            .chain(self.c2.to_compressed())
  249|       |            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
  250|       |        let cipher = Aes128Gcm::new(key);
  251|       |        let payload = aes_gcm::aead::Payload {
  252|       |            msg: &self.ciphertext[12..],
  253|       |            aad: &aad,
  254|       |        };
  255|       |        let plaintext = cipher
  256|       |            .decrypt(nonce, payload)
  257|      0|            .map_err(|_| Error::General("unable to decrypt arbitrary data: aes-128-gcm"))?;
  258|       |        let plaintext = String::from_utf8(plaintext)
  259|      0|            .map_err(|_| Error::General("unable to convert decrypted data to utf-8"))?;
  260|       |        let claim = ClaimData::from_text(&plaintext)?;
  261|       |        let computed_commitment = self.message_generator * claim.to_scalar();
  262|       |        if computed_commitment != expected_commitment {
  263|       |            return Err(Error::General(
  264|       |                "Invalid decrypted data. Computed commitment does not match expected commitment.",
  265|       |            ));
  266|       |        }
  267|       |        Ok(claim)
  268|       |    }

/mnt/g/paperexper/retf/saib-retf/prototype/anoncred-rust/vendor/credx-0.2.1-saib/src/statement/verifiable_encryption_decryption.rs:
    1|       |use crate::statement::Statement;
    2|       |use blsful::*;
    3|       |use elliptic_curve::group::GroupEncoding;
    4|       |use elliptic_curve::Group;
    5|       |use elliptic_curve_tools::group;
    6|       |use merlin::Transcript;
    7|       |use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;
    8|       |use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
    9|       |use uint_zigzag::Uint;
   10|       |
   11|       |/// Verifiable encryption that also allows decryption.
   12|       |///
   13|       |/// Use this statement when complete decryption is desired.
   14|       |/// The resulting proof is much larger than the one from [`VerifiableEncryptionStatement`]
   15|       |/// and slightly slower to generate.
   16|       |///
   17|       |/// The value encrypted and decrypted is a scalar value and not
   18|       |/// the original value. This is not meant to encrypt arbitrary data since
   19|       |/// arbitrary data can be of any length and cannot be proven in zero-knowledge.
   20|       |/// Instead, this is meant to encrypt a scalar value that can be proven in zero-knowledge
   21|       |/// and that's the best that can be achieved.
   22|       |///
   23|       |/// When arbitrary data is allowed,
   24|       |/// we use the proof to generate an AES key to encrypt
   25|       |/// arbitrary data then prove the encryption key is correct. But, again, this
   26|       |/// doesn't prove the encrypted data is correct. For that, verifiable decryption
   27|       |/// is needed. So the ciphertext when decrypted is proven to be correct but
   28|       |/// verifiers don't usually decrypt the data.
   29|      0|#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   30|       |pub struct VerifiableEncryptionDecryptionStatement<
   31|       |    P: Group + GroupEncoding + Serialize + DeserializeOwned,
   32|       |> {
   33|       |    /// The generator for the message element
   34|       |    #[serde(with = "group")]
   35|       |    pub message_generator: P,
   36|       |    /// The encryption key for this ciphertext
   37|       |    pub encryption_key: PublicKey<Bls12381G2Impl>,
   38|       |    /// The statement id
   39|       |    pub id: String,
   40|       |    /// The other statement id
   41|       |    pub reference_id: String,
   42|       |    /// The claim index in the other statement
   43|       |    pub claim: usize,
   44|       |}
   45|       |
   46|       |impl<P: Group + GroupEncoding + DeserializeOwned + Serialize> Statement
   47|       |    for VerifiableEncryptionDecryptionStatement<P>
   48|       |{
   49|      0|    fn id(&self) -> String {
   50|      0|        self.id.clone()
   51|      0|    }
   52|       |
   53|      0|    fn reference_ids(&self) -> Vec<String> {
   54|      0|        vec![self.reference_id.clone()]
   55|      0|    }
   56|       |
   57|      0|    fn add_challenge_contribution(&self, transcript: &mut Transcript) {
   58|      0|        transcript.append_message(
   59|      0|            b"statement type",
   60|      0|            b"el-gamal verifiable encryption w/decryption",
   61|      0|        );
   62|      0|        transcript.append_message(b"statement id", self.id.as_bytes());
   63|      0|        transcript.append_message(b"reference statement id", self.reference_id.as_bytes());
